{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/ReceivingScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, SafeAreaView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { BigButton } from '../components/BigButton';\nimport { addReceivedQty, findOpenItemByBarcode, OrderItem } from '../db/orders';\nimport { enqueue } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { findProduct, Product } from '../db/products';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';\nimport { colors } from '../theme/colors';\nimport { fontSize, radius, spacing } from '../theme/spacing';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Receiving'>;\n\nexport function ReceivingScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const { barcode } = route.params;\n  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);\n  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));\n      setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));\n    })();\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  const bump = (n: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setQty((q) => Math.max(1, q + n));\n  };\n\n  const confirm = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      const id = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;\n      const payload = {\n        id,\n        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,\n        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',\n        qty,\n        scanned_at: Date.now(),\n      };\n      await enqueue('receipt', payload);\n      if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.replace('Scanner');\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;\n  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;\n  const mismatch = expected !== null && alreadyIn + qty > expected;\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>\n      <View style={styles.card}>\n        <Text style={styles.name}>{product?.name ?? 'Unknown product'}</Text>\n        <Text style={styles.meta}>Barcode: {barcode}</Text>\n        {expected !== null && (\n          <Text style={[styles.expected, mismatch && { color: colors.danger }]}>\n            Expected {expected} · received {alreadyIn} so far\n          </Text>\n        )}\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.qtyRow}>\n        <Pressable style={styles.qtyBtn} onPress={() => bump(-1)}>\n          <Text style={styles.qtyBtnText}>−</Text>\n        </Pressable>\n        <Text style={styles.qtyNum}>{qty}</Text>\n        <Pressable style={styles.qtyBtn} onPress={() => bump(1)}>\n          <Text style={styles.qtyBtnText}>+</Text>\n        </Pressable>\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.quickRow}>\n        {[5, 10, 20].map((n) => (\n          <Pressable key={n} style={styles.quickBtn} onPress={() => { haptic.tap(); setQty(n); }}>\n            <Text style={styles.quickText}>{n}</Text>\n          </Pressable>\n        ))}\n      </View>\n\n      {mismatch && <Text style={styles.warn}>⚠ More than expected</Text>}\n\n      <View style={styles.footer}>\n        <BigButton\n          label={saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Confirm received'}\n          icon=\"✓\"\n          variant=\"success\"\n          onPress={confirm}\n          disabled={saving}\n        />\n        <BigButton label=\"Cancel\" variant=\"muted\" onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()} />\n      </View>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, padding: spacing.lg, gap: spacing.lg },\n  card: {\n    padding: spacing.lg,\n    backgroundColor: colors.bgMuted,\n    borderRadius: radius.lg,\n  },\n  name: { fontSize: fontSize.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },\n  meta: { fontSize: fontSize.sm, color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: spacing.xs },\n  expected: { marginTop: spacing.md, fontSize: fontSize.md, color: colors.text, fontWeight: '600' },\n  qtyRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: spacing.lg },\n  qtyBtn: {\n    width: 88,\n    height: 88,\n    borderRadius: 44,\n    backgroundColor: colors.primary,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  qtyBtnText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: 48, fontWeight: '800', lineHeight: 52 },\n  qtyNum: { fontSize: 72, fontWeight: '900', color: colors.text, minWidth: 120, textAlign: 'center' },\n  quickRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', gap: spacing.md },\n  quickBtn: {\n    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    borderRadius: radius.lg,\n    borderWidth: 2,\n    borderColor: colors.border,\n  },\n  quickText: { fontSize: fontSize.lg, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },\n  warn: { color: colors.danger, fontSize: fontSize.md, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center' },\n  footer: { gap: spacing.md, marginTop: 'auto' },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/OutboxScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { FlatList, RefreshControl, SafeAreaView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { BigButton } from '../components/BigButton';\nimport { getDb } from '../db/database';\nimport { OutboxRow } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';\nimport { colors } from '../theme/colors';\nimport { fontSize, radius, spacing } from '../theme/spacing';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Outbox'>;\n\nexport function OutboxScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const [rows, setRows] = useState<OutboxRow[]>([]);\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    const db = await getDb();\n    const list = await db.getAllAsync<OutboxRow>(\n      'SELECT * FROM outbox ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 100'\n    );\n    setRows(list);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n  }, [load]);\n\n  const sync = async () => {\n    setBusy(true);\n    try {\n      await flushOnce();\n      await load();\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>\n      <Text style={styles.h1}>Sync queue</Text>\n      <FlatList\n        data={rows}\n        keyExtractor={(r) => r.id}\n        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={busy} onRefresh={sync} />}\n        renderItem={({ item }) => (\n          <View style={styles.row}>\n            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n              <Text style={styles.rowTitle}>{item.kind}</Text>\n              <Text style={styles.rowMeta}>\n                {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()} · attempts {item.attempts}\n              </Text>\n              {item.last_error && <Text style={styles.err}>{item.last_error}</Text>}\n            </View>\n            <View style={[styles.badge, badgeStyle(item.status)]}>\n              <Text style={styles.badgeText}>{item.status}</Text>\n            </View>\n          </View>\n        )}\n        ListEmptyComponent={<Text style={styles.empty}>Nothing in the queue.</Text>}\n        contentContainerStyle={{ gap: spacing.sm, paddingBottom: spacing.lg }}\n      />\n      <BigButton label={busy ? 'Syncing…' : 'Sync now'} icon=\"🔄\" onPress={sync} disabled={busy} />\n      <BigButton label=\"Back\" variant=\"muted\" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction badgeStyle(status: OutboxRow['status']) {\n  switch (status) {\n    case 'sent':\n      return { backgroundColor: colors.success };\n    case 'failed':\n      return { backgroundColor: colors.danger };\n    case 'sending':\n      return { backgroundColor: colors.warn };\n    default:\n      return { backgroundColor: colors.textMuted };\n  }\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, padding: spacing.lg, gap: spacing.md },\n  h1: { fontSize: fontSize.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },\n  row: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    padding: spacing.md,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    backgroundColor: colors.bgMuted,\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n  },\n  rowTitle: { fontSize: fontSize.md, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },\n  rowMeta: { fontSize: fontSize.sm, color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: 2 },\n  err: { fontSize: fontSize.sm, color: colors.danger, marginTop: spacing.xs },\n  badge: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm, paddingVertical: spacing.xs, borderRadius: radius.sm },\n  badgeText: { color: '#fff', fontSize: fontSize.sm, fontWeight: '700' },\n  empty: { textAlign: 'center', color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: spacing.xl, fontSize: fontSize.md },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/OrdersScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { FlatList, SafeAreaView, StyleSheet, Text, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { BigButton } from '../components/BigButton';\nimport { getDb } from '../db/database';\nimport { getOrderItems, listOpenOrders, Order, OrderItem } from '../db/orders';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { colors } from '../theme/colors';\nimport { fontSize, radius, spacing } from '../theme/spacing';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Orders'>;\n\ntype OrderWithItems = Order & { items: OrderItem[] };\n\nexport function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<OrderWithItems[]>([]);\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    await getDb();\n    const list = await listOpenOrders();\n    const enriched = await Promise.all(\n      list.map(async (o) => ({ ...o, items: await getOrderItems(o.id) }))\n    );\n    setOrders(enriched);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n  }, [load]);\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>\n      <Text style={styles.h1}>Expected deliveries</Text>\n      <FlatList\n        data={orders}\n        keyExtractor={(o) => o.id}\n        renderItem={({ item }) => (\n          <View style={styles.card}>\n            <Text style={styles.vendor}>{item.vendor ?? 'Order'}</Text>\n            {item.expected_at && (\n              <Text style={styles.meta}>Due: {new Date(item.expected_at).toLocaleDateString()}</Text>\n            )}\n            <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />\n            {item.items.map((i) => {\n              const done = i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty;\n              return (\n                <View key={i.id} style={styles.itemRow}>\n                  <Text style={[styles.itemName, done && { color: colors.success }]}>\n                    {done ? '✓ ' : ''}{i.product_name}\n                  </Text>\n                  <Text style={styles.itemQty}>\n                    {i.received_qty} / {i.expected_qty}\n                  </Text>\n                </View>\n              );\n            })}\n          </View>\n        )}\n        ListEmptyComponent={\n          <Text style={styles.empty}>\n            No expected deliveries. Orders sync from the server when bills are processed.\n          </Text>\n        }\n        contentContainerStyle={{ gap: spacing.md, paddingBottom: spacing.lg }}\n      />\n      <BigButton label=\"Back\" variant=\"muted\" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.bg, padding: spacing.lg, gap: spacing.md },\n  h1: { fontSize: fontSize.xl, fontWeight: '800', color: colors.text },\n  card: { padding: spacing.md, backgroundColor: colors.bgMuted, borderRadius: radius.md },\n  vendor: { fontSize: fontSize.lg, fontWeight: '700', color: colors.text },\n  meta: { fontSize: fontSize.sm, color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: 2 },\n  itemRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: spacing.xs },\n  itemName: { fontSize: fontSize.md, color: colors.text, flex: 1 },\n  itemQty: { fontSize: fontSize.md, color: colors.text, fontWeight: '700' },\n  empty: { textAlign: 'center', color: colors.textMuted, marginTop: spacing.xl, fontSize: fontSize.md, paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg },\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

Phase 1 of the field app is in `field-app/`. It's self-contained — no workspace links to the umbrella repo, has its own `package.json`, `app.json`, `tsconfig.json`, and `.gitignore`. You can `cp -r field-app /path/to/new-repo && git init` whenever you want to split it out.

### What's implemented

- **Scan → Receive** flow: camera scans EAN/UPC/Code128/QR via `expo-camera`. Known barcode → quantity entry screen (big ± buttons, quick 5/10/20 chips, mismatch warning vs expected qty). Unknown barcode → product registration (name + category chips + unit chips).
- **SQLite outbox** (`expo-sqlite`): every receipt and product registration writes locally first, then a background flusher (`syncService.ts`, 30 s interval + on-demand) posts to `POST /v1/capture/{receipt,product}` with `X-Device-Id` / `X-Site-Id` headers. Survives offline; the **Sync queue** screen shows queued / sending / sent / failed rows with last error.
- **Orders screen** showing expected deliveries with received-vs-expected progress (populated server-side from bill extraction; pull-to-sync stub is there to extend).
- **Low-literacy UX primitives**: `BigButton`, haptics on every tap/success/warn, large icon-first labels, green-when-synced status badge on Home.
- App boots through `App.tsx` which opens the DB, kicks off sync, then mounts the navigator.

### Steps to install and test

```bash
cd field-app
npm install                                # installs Expo SDK 51 + deps already in package.json
export EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL="http://10.0.2.2:8080"   # Android emulator → host laptop
export EXPO_PUBLIC_SITE_ID="site-dev"
npm start                                  # Metro / Expo dev server
```

Then on your phone install **Expo Go** (Android, Play Store) and scan the QR code Metro prints, **or** press `a` in the Metro terminal to launch on a connected Android device/emulator. iOS works in Expo Go too if you want a quick look, but Android is the target.

Type-check at any point: `npm run lint`.

**Golden-path test (no backend needed — it queues offline):**
1. Open app → green "✓ All synced" badge.
2. Tap **Scan Item** → allow camera → point at any barcode in your kitchen.
3. Unknown barcode → fill name (e.g. "Tata Salt 1kg"), tap a category + unit, **Save & continue**.
4. **Receive item** screen → set qty with ± / 5 / 10 / 20 → **Confirm received**.
5. Home badge now says "1 waiting to send". Tap **Sync queue** to see the row (it will be `failed` if no backend is reachable — expected; pull-to-refresh retries).
6. Scan the same barcode again → skips registration, goes straight to receiving with the product pre-filled. That's the data flywheel.

**Graduate from Expo Go** (run `npm run prebuild && npm run android`) when you add ML Kit, BLE, Wi-Fi SSID, or WorkManager — that's Phase 2 from the architecture doc. For Phase 1, Expo Go's `expo-camera` barcode scanner is good enough to validate the workflow at one site.

### Server contract you need to implement next

- `POST /v1/capture/receipt` — `{ id, order_id, order_item_id, barcode, product_name, qty, scanned_at }`, idempotent on `id`.
- `POST /v1/capture/product` — `{ barcode, name, category, unit }`.
- Both with headers `X-Device-Id`, `X-Site-Id`.

Until that endpoint exists in `office-ops-engine`, the app still works fully offline; rows just stay in the outbox.

> DEVELOPER

what does these two lines mean? export EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL="http://10.0.2.2:8080"   # Android emulator → host laptop
  export EXPO_PUBLIC_SITE_ID="site-dev"

> AGENT

Those two lines are setting environment variables that the app reads at build time (via `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_*` in `src/config.ts`).

**`EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL="http://10.0.2.2:8080"`**

Tells the app where to POST receipts and product registrations.

- `10.0.2.2` is a magic IP that the **Android emulator** maps to your laptop's `localhost`. From inside the emulator, `127.0.0.1` would mean the emulator itself, not your machine — so Google reserved `10.0.2.2` to mean "the host computer running the emulator."
- `:8080` is the port your backend (`office-ops-engine`) listens on. Change it if your server runs on a different port.

For other setups you'd use a different value:

| Where the app runs | What to set |
|---|---|
| Android emulator → backend on your laptop | `http://10.0.2.2:8080` |
| iOS simulator → backend on your laptop | `http://localhost:8080` |
| Physical phone (Expo Go) on same Wi-Fi | `http://192.168.X.Y:8080` (your laptop's LAN IP — find via `ipconfig getifaddr en0`) |
| Deployed staging server | `https://api-staging.example.com` |

**`EXPO_PUBLIC_SITE_ID="site-dev"`**

Identifies which physical site this device belongs to (e.g. a specific office, warehouse, gate). The app sends it as the `X-Site-Id` header so the server knows which site's orders/products to scope to. `site-dev` is just a placeholder for local development — in production each device would be provisioned with the real site id.

**Why the `EXPO_PUBLIC_` prefix?** Expo only inlines env vars into the JS bundle if they start with `EXPO_PUBLIC_`. Anything without that prefix is treated as server-side / secret and won't reach the device. That's also a warning: never put real secrets (API keys, tokens) in `EXPO_PUBLIC_*` — they ship in the APK.

**If you skip them**, `src/config.ts` falls back to `http://10.0.2.2:8080` and `site-dev` defaults, so the app still runs — sync attempts will just fail (visible in the Sync queue screen) until a backend is reachable.

Node.js v24.14.1

> AGENT

Classic macOS Metro problem — the default file-watcher hits the OS open-files limit because your repo (and `node_modules`) has tens of thousands of files. Two fixes, do **either** (watchman is the recommended one):

### Fix 1: Install Watchman (recommended)

Metro auto-uses Watchman if it's installed, and Watchman doesn't hit this limit.

```bash
brew install watchman
```

Then in `field-app/`:

```bash
# kill the dead Metro
# (Ctrl+C in that terminal, or `pkill -f "expo start"`)

watchman watch-del-all          # clear any stale watches
npm start -- --clear            # restart Metro with a fresh cache
```

That's usually the whole fix.

### Fix 2: Raise the open-file limit for this shell

If you don't want Watchman, bump the limit before starting Metro. In your fish shell:

```fish
ulimit -n 65536
npm start
```

You can make it permanent by adding `ulimit -n 65536` to `~/.config/fish/config.fish`.

### Why this happened

Metro's fallback watcher (`NodeWatcher`) opens one file descriptor per watched file. macOS defaults to ~256 per process. Your `field-app/node_modules` after `npm install` has way more than that, so the first time Metro tries to watch them all, it errors out with `EMFILE`. Watchman uses kernel-level FSEvents instead and watches the whole tree with a handful of descriptors.

Install watchman, restart Metro, and the QR code should come up cleanly.

> DEVELOPER

I scanned the QR on expo go, it scanned and nothing happened

> AGENT

Metro is in **dev-client mode**, not Expo Go mode — that's why Expo Go silently does nothing when it scans. Look at the first line of your output:

```
exp+field-app://expo-development-client/?url=...
```

That `expo-development-client` URL scheme is only understood by a custom dev build of the app installed on the device. Expo Go doesn't recognize it, so the scan succeeds but it can't launch anything.

The cause is in your `package.json`:

```json
"start": "expo start --dev-client"
```

The `--dev-client` flag tells Metro to advertise the dev-client URL.

### Fix — switch Metro to Expo Go

Two easy options:

**Option A (one-time, no file change):** in the Metro terminal that's already running, press **`s`**. You'll see it flip to "Using Expo Go" and reprint a new QR. Rescan that one with Expo Go — it'll load.

**Option B (permanent):** change the start script so plain `npm start` targets Expo Go.

  "new_string": "    \"start\": \"expo start\",\n    \"start:dev-client\": \"expo start --dev-client\","
}

### Heads-up before you rescan

`expo-dev-client` is in your dependencies but **only a no-op inside Expo Go**. The app will still load — you may just see a warning in the logs about it not being a dev build. Ignore it for now. If it actually errors out on launch, remove it:

```bash
npm uninstall expo-dev-client
```

After pressing `s` (or restarting with `npm start`), rescan with Expo Go — you should see the **Field App** home screen with the green "✓ All synced" badge.

> AGENT

Your phone's Expo Go is on SDK 54 (current) but I wrote the project for SDK 51. The fastest fix is to bump the project to SDK 54 — Expo's CLI will pin all peer deps for you.

### Upgrade in place

```bash
cd field-app
npm install expo@^54
npx expo install --fix
```

What those do:
- `npm install expo@^54` upgrades the Expo SDK itself.
- `npx expo install --fix` walks every Expo-related dep in `package.json` (`expo-camera`, `expo-sqlite`, `expo-haptics`, `react-native`, `react`, `react-native-reanimated`, `react-native-screens`, etc.) and rewrites them to the exact versions SDK 54 expects. This is the supported upgrade path — don't hand-edit version strings, you'll hit peer-dep mismatches.

Then clear caches and restart:

```bash
watchman watch-del-all
rm -rf node_modules .expo
npm install
npm start -- --clear
```

Rescan the QR with Expo Go — it should load.

### One thing to watch after the bump

SDK 54 ships React Native 0.81 + React 19. A couple of API touch-ups may be needed:

- `expo-sqlite` — the `openDatabaseAsync` / `execAsync` / `getFirstAsync` / `getAllAsync` / `runAsync` API I used is the modern one and is still current in SDK 54, so `src/db/*` should be fine.
- `expo-camera` — `CameraView` + `useCameraPermissions` + `onBarcodeScanned` is also the current API.
- `react-native-reanimated` v4 needs the worklets package; `npx expo install --fix` will add `react-native-worklets` automatically. Just make sure `babel.config.js` still has `'react-native-reanimated/plugin'` (it does).

If anything red shows up on launch, paste the error and I'll patch it. Otherwise the alternative (install older Expo Go for SDK 51 via the link Expo printed) works but you'd be stuck on an old SDK from day one — not worth it.

> AGENT

The error is a stale `node_modules` issue, not a real version conflict. Your `package.json` is already correct for SDK 54, but the old `expo-camera@15` (and friends) are still sitting in `node_modules` from the SDK 51 install. When npm tries to layer the new versions on top, it sees the old ones as "installed" and gets confused about the React 19 peer.

Wipe and reinstall from scratch:

```bash
cd field-app
rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json .expo
npm install
npx expo install --fix    # should now report "Dependencies are up to date"
```

Then start Metro fresh:

```bash
watchman watch-del-all
npm start -- --clear
```

### If `npm install` still complains

Some SDK 54 peer ranges are genuinely tight around React 19. If a clean install still errors, fall back to:

```bash
npm install --legacy-peer-deps
```

This is the officially-supported workaround for SDK 54 + npm — Expo's docs explicitly recommend it for npm users because npm's peer-dep resolver is stricter than yarn/pnpm. It doesn't mean anything is actually broken; it just tells npm to trust the resolution Expo chose. (If you'd rather not, `pnpm install` or `yarn install` also work and don't need the flag.)

After install succeeds, rescan with Expo Go SDK 54 — the home screen should come up.

`babel-preset-expo@^56.0.12` is the wrong version — that's an off-track release. SDK 54 wants a specific matching version. Let Expo pick:

```bash
cd /Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app
npx expo install babel-preset-expo
npm start -- --clear
```

`npx expo install` (vs `npm install`) reads your installed Expo SDK and pins the babel preset version that ships with it. That fixes the syntax error — the old preset doesn't know how to compile modern JS class private fields (`#field`) that show up in SDK 54's runtime code.

If after that `npx expo install --fix` reports any other version mismatches, run it too — same idea, it'll align everything to SDK 54's expected versions.

Yes — Metro caches the transformed bundle aggressively, and `node_modules` may still have the wrong `babel-preset-expo` nested somewhere. Nuke everything:

```bash
cd /Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app

# kill any running Metro first (Ctrl+C in that terminal)

rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json .expo
rm -rf $TMPDIR/metro-* $TMPDIR/haste-map-* 2>/dev/null

npm install
npx expo install --fix      # aligns ALL deps incl. babel-preset-expo
watchman watch-del-all
npm start -- --clear
```

After the install, check what version of `babel-preset-expo` actually landed:

```bash
npm ls babel-preset-expo
```

For SDK 54 it should print something like `babel-preset-expo@~13.x` or `~14.x` — **not** 56. If it's still showing 56, your `package.json` has it pinned wrong; tell me what `npm ls` prints and I'll fix the version explicitly.

Also worth confirming Metro is actually using your `babel.config.js`. Run this once before `npm start`:

```bash
cat babel.config.js
```

Should be exactly:

```js
module.exports = function (api) {
  api.cache(true);
  return {
    presets: ['babel-preset-expo'],
    plugins: ['react-native-reanimated/plugin'],
  };
};
```

If that all looks right and you still get the `private properties` syntax error, paste the `npm ls babel-preset-expo` output.

> AGENT

I'll rebuild the UI around a real design system (Material 3 + Google-style hierarchy/motion, tuned for low-literacy operators on small devices), then tighten the scanner.

### Design language (what I'm committing to)

- **One source of truth** in `src/design/tokens.ts` — color roles (primary / surface / container / error / outline), type scale, spacing, radius, elevation, motion durations. Every component reads from this. No more inline magic numbers.
- **A small set of primitives** (`Text`, `Surface`, `Button`, `IconButton`, `Card`, `AppBar`, `TextField`, `Chip`, `ListItem`). Every screen composes these. New components added later must follow the same prop shape (variant, size, leading/trailing icon).
- **Hierarchy**: one display-size element per screen, one primary action per screen, tonal/secondary actions step down in weight.
- **Feedback**: every tap → 100ms scale + haptic. Every success → green check + success haptic. Every destructive action → tonal red, not loud red. (Google UX: surfaces talk, they don't shout.)
- **Motion**: enter/exit animations on screen transitions (handled by the stack navigator), pressed-state scale on every touchable.

Let me write the system and rewrite every screen.

lg: 16,\n  xl: 24,\n  pill: 999,\n} as const;\n\nconst sysFont = Platform.select({\n  ios: 'System',\n  android: 'sans-serif',\n  default: 'System',\n})!;\n\nconst sysFontMedium = Platform.select({\n  ios: 'System',\n  android: 'sans-serif-medium',\n  default: 'System',\n})!;\n\ntype TypeVariant = {\n  fontFamily: string;\n  fontSize: number;\n  lineHeight: number;\n  fontWeight: TextStyle['fontWeight'];\n  letterSpacing?: number;\n};\n\nexport const type: Record<\n  | 'displayLarge'\n  | 'displayMedium'\n  | 'headlineLarge'\n  | 'headlineMedium'\n  | 'headlineSmall'\n  | 'titleLarge'\n  | 'titleMedium'\n  | 'bodyLarge'\n  | 'bodyMedium'\n  | 'labelLarge'\n  | 'labelMedium',\n  TypeVariant\n> = {\n  displayLarge: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 56, lineHeight: 64, fontWeight: '800', letterSpacing: -0.5 },\n  displayMedium: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 44, lineHeight: 52, fontWeight: '800', letterSpacing: -0.25 },\n  headlineLarge: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 32, lineHeight: 40, fontWeight: '700' },\n  headlineMedium: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 28, lineHeight: 36, fontWeight: '700' },\n  headlineSmall: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 24, lineHeight: 32, fontWeight: '700' },\n  titleLarge: { fontFamily: sysFontMedium, fontSize: 22, lineHeight: 28, fontWeight: '600' },\n  titleMedium: { fontFamily: sysFontMedium, fontSize: 16, lineHeight: 24, fontWeight: '600', letterSpacing: 0.15 },\n  bodyLarge: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 17, lineHeight: 24, fontWeight: '400', letterSpacing: 0.15 },\n  bodyMedium: { fontFamily: sysFont, fontSize: 14, lineHeight: 20, fontWeight: '400', letterSpacing: 0.25 },\n  labelLarge: { fontFamily: sysFontMedium, fontSize: 14, lineHeight: 20, fontWeight: '600', letterSpacing: 0.1 },\n  labelMedium: { fontFamily: sysFontMedium, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 16, fontWeight: '600', letterSpacing: 0.5 },\n};\n\nexport const elevation = {\n  level0: {\n    shadowColor: 'transparent',\n    shadowOpacity: 0,\n    shadowRadius: 0,\n    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 0 },\n    elevation: 0,\n  },\n  level1: {\n    shadowColor: '#000',\n    shadowOpacity: 0.08,\n    shadowRadius: 2,\n    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 1 },\n    elevation: 1,\n  },\n  level2: {\n    shadowColor: '#000',\n    shadowOpacity: 0.1,\n    shadowRadius: 6,\n    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },\n    elevation: 3,\n  },\n  level3: {\n    shadowColor: '#000',\n    shadowOpacity: 0.12,\n    shadowRadius: 12,\n    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 },\n    elevation: 6,\n  },\n} as const;\n\nexport const motion = {\n  fast: 120,\n  base: 200,\n  slow: 320,\n} as const;\n\nexport const hitSlop = { top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 };\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/Text.tsx",
  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { StyleProp, Text as RNText, TextProps, TextStyle } from 'react-native';\nimport { palette, type } from './tokens';\n\ntype Variant = keyof typeof type;\n\ntype Props = TextProps & {\n  variant?: Variant;\n  color?: string;\n  style?: StyleProp<TextStyle>;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport function Text({ variant = 'bodyLarge', color = palette.onSurface, style, children, ...rest }: Props) {\n  return (\n    <RNText {...rest} style={[type[variant], { color }, style]}>\n      {children}\n    </RNText>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/Surface.tsx",
  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { StyleProp, View, ViewProps, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';\nimport { elevation as elev, palette, radius } from './tokens';\n\ntype Tone = 'low' | 'base' | 'high' | 'highest' | 'lowest';\ntype Level = keyof typeof elev;\n\nconst toneToColor: Record<Tone, string> = {\n  lowest: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n  low: palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n  base: palette.surfaceContainer,\n  high: palette.surfaceContainerHigh,\n  highest: palette.surfaceContainerHighest,\n};\n\ntype Props = ViewProps & {\n  tone?: Tone;\n  level?: Level;\n  rounded?: keyof typeof radius;\n  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;\n  children?: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport function Surface({\n  tone = 'lowest',\n  level = 'level0',\n  rounded = 'lg',\n  style,\n  children,\n  ...rest\n}: Props) {\n  return (\n    <View\n      {...rest}\n      style={[\n        { backgroundColor: toneToColor[tone], borderRadius: radius[rounded] },\n        elev[level],\n        style,\n      ]}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/Button.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { useRef } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Animated,\n  Pressable,\n  StyleProp,\n  StyleSheet,\n  View,\n  ViewStyle,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { motion, palette, radius, spacing } from './tokens';\n\nexport type ButtonVariant = 'filled' | 'tonal' | 'outlined' | 'text' | 'danger';\nexport type ButtonSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';\n\ntype Props = {\n  label: string;\n  onPress: () => void;\n  variant?: ButtonVariant;\n  size?: ButtonSize;\n  leadingIcon?: React.ReactNode;\n  trailingIcon?: React.ReactNode;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n  loading?: boolean;\n  fullWidth?: boolean;\n  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;\n  testID?: string;\n};\n\ntype Tokens = { bg: string; fg: string; border: string; pressedBg: string };\n\nfunction variantTokens(v: ButtonVariant, disabled: boolean): Tokens {\n  if (disabled) {\n    return {\n      bg: palette.surfaceContainerHighest,\n      fg: palette.onSurfaceVariant,\n      border: 'transparent',\n      pressedBg: palette.surfaceContainerHighest,\n    };\n  }\n  switch (v) {\n    case 'filled':\n      return {\n        bg: palette.primary,\n        fg: palette.onPrimary,\n        border: 'transparent',\n        pressedBg: '#08522F',\n      };\n    case 'tonal':\n      return {\n        bg: palette.primaryContainer,\n        fg: palette.onPrimaryContainer,\n        border: 'transparent',\n        pressedBg: '#87DBA9',\n      };\n    case 'outlined':\n      return {\n        bg: 'transparent',\n        fg: palette.primary,\n        border: palette.outline,\n        pressedBg: palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n      };\n    case 'text':\n      return {\n        bg: 'transparent',\n        fg: palette.primary,\n        border: 'transparent',\n        pressedBg: palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n      };\n    case 'danger':\n      return {\n        bg: palette.errorContainer,\n        fg: palette.onErrorContainer,\n        border: 'transparent',\n        pressedBg: '#FFB4AB',\n      };\n  }\n}\n\nconst SIZES: Record<ButtonSize, { h: number; px: number; gap: number; variant: 'labelLarge' | 'titleMedium' | 'titleLarge' | 'headlineSmall' }> = {\n  sm: { h: 40, px: spacing.lg, gap: spacing.sm, variant: 'labelLarge' },\n  md: { h: 52, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm, variant: 'titleMedium' },\n  lg: { h: 64, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.md, variant: 'titleLarge' },\n  xl: { h: 88, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.md, variant: 'headlineSmall' },\n};\n\nexport function Button({\n  label,\n  onPress,\n  variant = 'filled',\n  size = 'md',\n  leadingIcon,\n  trailingIcon,\n  disabled,\n  loading,\n  fullWidth,\n  style,\n  testID,\n}: Props) {\n  const scale = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;\n  const t = variantTokens(variant, !!disabled || !!loading);\n  const s = SIZES[size];\n\n  const animateTo = (v: number) =>\n    Animated.timing(scale, {\n      toValue: v,\n      duration: motion.fast,\n      useNativeDriver: true,\n    }).start();\n\n  return (\n    <Animated.View style={[fullWidth && { alignSelf: 'stretch' }, { transform: [{ scale }] }, style]}>\n      <Pressable\n        testID={testID}\n        disabled={disabled || loading}\n        onPressIn={() => animateTo(0.97)}\n        onPressOut={() => animateTo(1)}\n        onPress={() => {\n          haptic.tap();\n          onPress();\n        }}\n        android_ripple={{ color: t.pressedBg, borderless: false }}\n        style={({ pressed }) => [\n          styles.base,\n          {\n            minHeight: s.h,\n            paddingHorizontal: s.px,\n            backgroundColor: pressed ? t.pressedBg : t.bg,\n            borderColor: t.border,\n            borderWidth: variant === 'outlined' ? 1.5 : 0,\n            opacity: disabled ? 0.6 : 1,\n          },\n        ]}\n      >\n        <View style={[styles.row, { gap: s.gap }]}>\n          {leadingIcon ? <View>{leadingIcon}</View> : null}\n          <Text variant={s.variant} color={t.fg}>\n            {loading ? 'Working…' : label}\n          </Text>\n          {trailingIcon ? <View>{trailingIcon}</View> : null}\n        </View>\n      </Pressable>\n    </Animated.View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  base: {\n    borderRadius: radius.pill,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },\n});\n"
}

  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleProp, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { hitSlop, palette, radius } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = {\n  icon: string;\n  onPress: () => void;\n  size?: number;\n  tone?: 'default' | 'inverse';\n  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;\n};\n\nexport function IconButton({ icon, onPress, size = 44, tone = 'default', style }: Props) {\n  const color = tone === 'inverse' ? palette.surface : palette.onSurface;\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      hitSlop={hitSlop}\n      onPress={() => {\n        haptic.tap();\n        onPress();\n      }}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: size / 2 }}\n      style={[styles.base, { width: size, height: size, borderRadius: radius.pill }, style]}\n    >\n      <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" color={color}>\n        {icon}\n      </Text>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  base: { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },\n});\n"
}

Pressable : View;\n  return (\n    <Container\n      onPress={onPress}\n      android_ripple={onPress ? { color: palette.outlineVariant } : undefined}\n      style={[\n        {\n          backgroundColor:\n            tone === 'outlined'\n              ? palette.surface\n              : tone === 'elevated'\n              ? palette.surfaceContainerLowest\n              : palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n          borderRadius: radius.lg,\n          padding: spacing[padding],\n          borderWidth: tone === 'outlined' ? 1 : 0,\n          borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n          ...(tone === 'elevated'\n            ? {\n                shadowColor: '#000',\n                shadowOpacity: 0.08,\n                shadowRadius: 6,\n                shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },\n                elevation: 2,\n              }\n            : null),\n          overflow: 'hidden',\n        },\n        style,\n      ]}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </Container>\n  );\n}\n"

(\n            <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {subtitle}\n            </Text>\n          ) : null}\n        </View>\n        <View style={styles.trailing}>{trailing}</View>\n      </View>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { backgroundColor: palette.surface },\n  row: {\n    minHeight: 56,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm,\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n  },\n  leading: { width: 56, alignItems: 'flex-start' },\n  trailing: { minWidth: 56, alignItems: 'flex-end', paddingRight: spacing.sm },\n  titleWrap: { flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm },\n});\n"

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/TextField.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, TextInput, TextInputProps, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { palette, radius, spacing, type as typo } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = TextInputProps & {\n  label: string;\n  helper?: string;\n  error?: string;\n};\n\nexport function TextField({ label, helper, error, onFocus, onBlur, style, ...rest }: Props) {\n  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);\n  const borderColor = error\n    ? palette.error\n    : focused\n    ? palette.primary\n    : palette.outlineVariant;\n  return (\n    <View>\n      <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.xs }}>\n        {label}\n      </Text>\n      <TextInput\n        {...rest}\n        onFocus={(e) => {\n          setFocused(true);\n          onFocus?.(e);\n        }}\n        onBlur={(e) => {\n          setFocused(false);\n          onBlur?.(e);\n        }}\n        placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n        style={[\n          styles.input,\n          typo.bodyLarge,\n          { borderColor, color: palette.onSurface, borderWidth: focused ? 2 : 1.5 },\n          style,\n        ]}\n      />\n      {(error || helper) && (\n        <Text\n          variant=\"labelMedium\"\n          color={error ? palette.error : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}\n        >\n          {error ?? helper}\n        </Text>\n      )}\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  input: {\n    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 56,\n  },\n});\n"
}

(\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSecondaryContainer}>\n            ✓\n          </Text>\n        ) : leadingIcon ?   "content": "import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { StatusPill } from '../design';\nimport { onPendingChange } from '../sync/syncService';\n\nexport function QueueBadge() {\n  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);\n  useEffect(() => onPendingChange(setCount), []);\n  if (count === 0) return <StatusPill label=\"All synced\" tone=\"success\" leadingIcon=\"✓\" />;\n  return <StatusPill label={`${count} waiting to send`} tone=\"warn\" leadingIcon=\"↻\" />;\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/HomeScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\nimport { QueueBadge } from '../components/QueueBadge';\nimport { Button, Card, palette, spacing, Text } from '../design';\nimport { listProducts } from '../db/products';\nimport { listOpenOrders } from '../db/orders';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Home'>;\n\nexport function HomeScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ products: 0, orders: 0 });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const load = async () => {\n      const [p, o] = await Promise.all([listProducts(1000), listOpenOrders()]);\n      setStats({ products: p.length, orders: o.length });\n    };\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation]);\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.safe}>\n      <View style={styles.header}>\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          GATE ENTRY\n        </Text>\n        <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n          Field\n        </Text>\n        <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n          <QueueBadge />\n        </View>\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.primary}>\n        <Button\n          label=\"Scan item\"\n          leadingIcon={<Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" color={palette.onPrimary}>⌖</Text>}\n          size=\"xl\"\n          fullWidth\n          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Scanner')}\n        />\n        <Text\n          variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n          color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}\n        >\n          Point camera at a barcode. We'll do the rest.\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.tiles}>\n        <Card\n          tone=\"filled\"\n          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Orders')}\n          style={styles.tile}\n        >\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            DELIVERIES\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {stats.orders}\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            expected today\n          </Text>\n        </Card>\n        <Card\n          tone=\"filled\"\n          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Outbox')}\n          style={styles.tile}\n        >\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            CATALOG\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {stats.products}\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            products mapped\n          </Text>\n        </Card>\n      </View>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  header: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.lg, paddingBottom: spacing.xl },\n  primary: {\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    paddingTop: spacing.md,\n    paddingBottom: spacing.xxl,\n  },\n  tiles: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    gap: spacing.md,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n  },\n  tile: { flex: 1 },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/ScannerScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';\nimport { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Animated,\n  Easing,\n  Platform,\n  Pressable,\n  StyleSheet,\n  View,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\nimport { Button, IconButton, palette, spacing, Text } from '../design';\nimport { findProduct } from '../db/products';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Scanner'>;\n\n// Only the formats we actually expect at the gate. Fewer formats = faster\n// scanning loop, especially on cheap Androids.\nconst FORMATS = ['ean13', 'ean8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e', 'code128', 'qr'] as const;\n\nexport function ScannerScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();\n  const [torch, setTorch] = useState(false);\n  const lockedRef = useRef(false);\n  const flashOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;\n  const linePos = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (permission && !permission.granted && permission.canAskAgain) requestPermission();\n  }, [permission, requestPermission]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const anim = Animated.loop(\n      Animated.sequence([\n        Animated.timing(linePos, { toValue: 1, duration: 1600, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease), useNativeDriver: true }),\n        Animated.timing(linePos, { toValue: 0, duration: 1600, easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease), useNativeDriver: true }),\n      ])\n    );\n    anim.start();\n    return () => anim.stop();\n  }, [linePos]);\n\n  const flash = useCallback(() => {\n    flashOpacity.setValue(0.6);\n    Animated.timing(flashOpacity, { toValue: 0, duration: 240, useNativeDriver: true }).start();\n  }, [flashOpacity]);\n\n  const handleScan = useCallback(\n    ({ data }: { data: string }) => {\n      if (lockedRef.current || !data) return;\n      lockedRef.current = true;\n      haptic.success();\n      flash();\n      // Fire-and-forget DB lookup, navigate as soon as we know the answer.\n      // Synchronous lock above already prevents duplicate scans.\n      findProduct(data)\n        .then((existing) => {\n          if (existing) navigation.replace('Receiving', { barcode: data });\n          else navigation.replace('RegisterProduct', { barcode: data });\n        })\n        .catch(() => {\n          // If lookup fails, fall back to registration to be safe.\n          navigation.replace('RegisterProduct', { barcode: data });\n        });\n    },\n    [navigation, flash]\n  );\n\n  const barcodeSettings = useMemo(\n    () => ({ barcodeTypes: [...FORMATS] }),\n    []\n  );\n\n  const lineY = linePos.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0, 220] });\n\n  if (!permission) {\n    return <View style={styles.center} />;\n  }\n\n  if (!permission.granted) {\n    return (\n      <SafeAreaView style={styles.permission}>\n        <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: spacing.md }}>\n          Camera access needed\n        </Text>\n        <Text\n          variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n          color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: spacing.xl }}\n        >\n          We use the camera only to read barcodes — no photos are taken.\n        </Text>\n        <Button label=\"Allow camera\" onPress={requestPermission} size=\"lg\" fullWidth />\n        <Button\n          label=\"Not now\"\n          variant=\"text\"\n          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n        />\n      </SafeAreaView>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.root}>\n      <CameraView\n        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}\n        facing=\"back\"\n        enableTorch={torch}\n        barcodeScannerSettings={barcodeSettings}\n        onBarcodeScanned={handleScan}\n        // @ts-ignore — Android-only prop, ignored elsewhere\n        autofocus=\"on\"\n      />\n\n      {/* Dim mask with a transparent window */}\n      <View style={styles.maskTop} />\n      <View style={styles.maskRow}>\n        <View style={styles.maskSide} />\n        <View style={styles.window}>\n          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTL]} />\n          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTR]} />\n          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBL]} />\n          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBR]} />\n          <Animated.View style={[styles.scanLine, { transform: [{ translateY: lineY }] }]} />\n        </View>\n        <View style={styles.maskSide} />\n      </View>\n      <View style={styles.maskBottom} />\n\n      <Animated.View pointerEvents=\"none\" style={[styles.flash, { opacity: flashOpacity }]} />\n\n      <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.topBar}>\n        <View style={styles.topBarRow}>\n          <IconButton icon=\"✕\" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} tone=\"inverse\" />\n          <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color=\"#fff\">\n            Scan barcode\n          </Text>\n          <IconButton\n            icon={torch ? '◉' : '○'}\n            onPress={() => setTorch((t) => !t)}\n            tone=\"inverse\"\n          />\n        </View>\n      </SafeAreaView>\n\n      <SafeAreaView edges={['bottom']} style={styles.hint}>\n        <Text\n          variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n          color=\"#fff\"\n          style={{ textAlign: 'center', paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl }}\n        >\n          Hold steady. Align the barcode inside the frame.\n        </Text>\n      </SafeAreaView>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst WINDOW = 260;\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },\n  center: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },\n  permission: {\n    flex: 1,\n    backgroundColor: palette.background,\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  topBar: { position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0 },\n  topBarRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'space-between',\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,\n  },\n  maskTop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },\n  maskBottom: { flex: 1.4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },\n  maskRow: { flexDirection: 'row', height: WINDOW },\n  maskSide: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },\n  window: { width: WINDOW, height: WINDOW, overflow: 'hidden' },\n  corner: {\n    position: 'absolute',\n    width: 28,\n    height: 28,\n    borderColor: '#fff',\n  },\n  cornerTL: { top: 0, left: 0, borderTopWidth: 3, borderLeftWidth: 3, borderTopLeftRadius: 12 },\n  cornerTR: { top: 0, right: 0, borderTopWidth: 3, borderRightWidth: 3, borderTopRightRadius: 12 },\n  cornerBL: { bottom: 0, left: 0, borderBottomWidth: 3, borderLeftWidth: 3, borderBottomLeftRadius: 12 },\n  cornerBR: { bottom: 0, right: 0, borderBottomWidth: 3, borderRightWidth: 3, borderBottomRightRadius: 12 },\n  scanLine: {\n    position: 'absolute',\n    left: 8,\n    right: 8,\n    height: 2,\n    backgroundColor: palette.primaryContainer,\n    shadowColor: palette.primaryContainer,\n    shadowOpacity: 1,\n    shadowRadius: 8,\n    ...(Platform.OS === 'android' ? { elevation: 4 } : null),\n  },\n  flash: {\n    ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject,\n    backgroundColor: '#fff',\n  },\n  hint: { position: 'absolute', bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, paddingBottom: spacing.lg },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/RegisterProductScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Chip,\n  palette,\n  spacing,\n  Text,\n  TextField,\n} from '../design';\nimport { enqueue } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { upsertProduct } from '../db/products';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'RegisterProduct'>;\n\nconst CATEGORIES = ['Grocery', 'Cleaning', 'Office', 'Cafeteria', 'Other'];\nconst UNITS = ['Piece', 'Kg', 'Litre', 'Pack', 'Box'];\n\nexport function RegisterProductScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const { barcode } = route.params;\n  const [name, setName] = useState('');\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Grocery');\n  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('Piece');\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const canSave = name.trim().length > 0 && !saving;\n\n  const onSave = async () => {\n    if (!canSave) {\n      haptic.warn();\n      return;\n    }\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await upsertProduct({ barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });\n      await enqueue('product_registration', { barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.replace('Receiving', { barcode });\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar title=\"New product\" subtitle={barcode} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n      <KeyboardAvoidingView\n        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}\n        style={{ flex: 1 }}\n      >\n        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll} keyboardShouldPersistTaps=\"handled\">\n          <Text variant=\"headlineMedium\">What is this?</Text>\n          <Text variant=\"bodyLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            Tell us once. We'll remember it next time you scan it.\n          </Text>\n\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>\n            <TextField\n              label=\"Product name\"\n              value={name}\n              onChangeText={setName}\n              placeholder=\"e.g. Tata Salt 1kg\"\n              autoFocus\n              returnKeyType=\"done\"\n            />\n          </View>\n\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>\n              CATEGORY\n            </Text>\n            <View style={styles.chipRow}>\n              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (\n                <Chip key={c} label={c} selected={c === category} onPress={() => setCategory(c)} />\n              ))}\n            </View>\n          </View>\n\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>\n              UNIT\n            </Text>\n            <View style={styles.chipRow}>\n              {UNITS.map((u) => (\n                <Chip key={u} label={u} selected={u === unit} onPress={() => setUnit(u)} />\n              ))}\n            </View>\n          </View>\n        </ScrollView>\n\n        <View style={styles.footer}>\n          <Button\n            label=\"Save & continue\"\n            onPress={onSave}\n            disabled={!canSave}\n            loading={saving}\n            size=\"lg\"\n            fullWidth\n          />\n          <Button\n            label=\"Cancel\"\n            variant=\"text\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}\n            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n          />\n        </View>\n      </KeyboardAvoidingView>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  scroll: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  chipRow: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: spacing.sm },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n  },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/screens/ReceivingScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Card,\n  palette,\n  radius,\n  spacing,\n  StatusPill,\n  Text,\n} from '../design';\nimport { addReceivedQty, findOpenItemByBarcode, OrderItem } from '../db/orders';\nimport { enqueue } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { findProduct, Product } from '../db/products';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Receiving'>;\n\nexport function ReceivingScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const { barcode } = route.params;\n  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);\n  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));\n      setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));\n    })();\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  const bump = (n: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setQty((q) => Math.max(1, q + n));\n  };\n  const setExact = (n: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setQty(n);\n  };\n\n  const confirm = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      const id = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;\n      await enqueue('receipt', {\n        id,\n        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,\n        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',\n        qty,\n        scanned_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.replace('Scanner');\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;\n  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;\n  const projected = alreadyIn + qty;\n  const mismatch = expected !== null && projected > expected;\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar\n        title=\"Receive item\"\n        onBack={() => navigation.popToTop()}\n        trailing={\n          expected !== null ? (\n            <StatusPill\n              label={`${projected} / ${expected}`}\n              tone={mismatch ? 'danger' : projected === expected ? 'success' : 'neutral'}\n            />\n          ) : null\n        }\n      />\n\n      <View style={styles.content}>\n        <Card tone=\"elevated\" padding=\"xl\">\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            PRODUCT\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {product?.name ?? 'Unknown product'}\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {barcode}\n          </Text>\n          {expected !== null ? (\n            <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n              Expected {expected} · {alreadyIn} already received\n            </Text>\n          ) : (\n            <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n              No matching order — capture stands alone.\n            </Text>\n          )}\n        </Card>\n\n        <View style={styles.qtySection}>\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n            QUANTITY RECEIVED\n          </Text>\n          <View style={styles.stepperRow}>\n            <Stepper icon=\"−\" onPress={() => bump(-1)} />\n            <Text variant=\"displayLarge\" style={styles.qtyNum}>\n              {qty}\n            </Text>\n            <Stepper icon=\"+\" onPress={() => bump(1)} />\n          </View>\n          <View style={styles.quickRow}>\n            {[5, 10, 20, 50].map((n) => (\n              <QuickChip key={n} value={n} active={qty === n} onPress={() => setExact(n)} />\n            ))}\n          </View>\n          {mismatch ? (\n            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n              <StatusPill\n                label={`Over by ${projected - expected!}`}\n                tone=\"danger\"\n                leadingIcon=\"⚠\"\n              />\n            </View>\n          ) : null}\n        </View>\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.footer}>\n        <Button\n          label=\"Confirm received\"\n          onPress={confirm}\n          loading={saving}\n          size=\"lg\"\n          fullWidth\n          leadingIcon={\n            <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" color={palette.onPrimary}>\n              ✓\n            </Text>\n          }\n        />\n        <Button\n          label=\"Cancel\"\n          variant=\"text\"\n          onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n        />\n      </View>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Stepper({ icon, onPress }: { icon: string; onPress: () => void }) {\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      onPress={onPress}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 44 }}\n      style={styles.stepper}\n    >\n      <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" color={palette.onPrimary}>\n        {icon}\n      </Text>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction QuickChip({ value, active, onPress }: { value: number; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      onPress={onPress}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}\n      style={[\n        styles.quickChip,\n        {\n          backgroundColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.surfaceContainerLow,\n          borderColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.outlineVariant,\n        },\n      ]}\n    >\n      <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={active ? palette.onSecondaryContainer : palette.onSurface}>\n        {value}\n      </Text>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  content: { flex: 1, padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },\n  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'stretch' },\n  stepperRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: spacing.xl },\n  stepper: {\n    width: 88,\n    height: 88,\n    borderRadius: 44,\n    backgroundColor: palette.primary,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  qtyNum: { minWidth: 120, textAlign: 'center' },\n  quickRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: spacing.sm, justifyContent: 'center' },\n  quickChip: {\n    minWidth: 64,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    borderRadius: radius.pill,\n    borderWidth: 1,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n  },\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

Type-check is clean. Restart Metro with `npm start -- --clear` and reload Expo Go.

### What changed (and why it's "the system" now)

**Design system in `src/design/`** — every component reads from `tokens.ts`. Add a screen → import from `design/` only. If you want to retheme to a different brand color, edit one line in `palette.primary` and the whole app moves.

- `tokens.ts` — M3-style color roles (primary/container/surface/error/outline), spacing scale, radius scale, type scale (display → label), elevation levels, motion durations.
- `Text` — typography primitive. Variants: `displayLarge`, `headlineMedium`, `titleLarge`, `bodyLarge`, `labelLarge`, etc. No more inline `fontSize`.
- `Button` — five variants (`filled`, `tonal`, `outlined`, `text`, `danger`), four sizes, leading/trailing icons, loading state, pressed-scale animation, Android ripple, haptic on every tap. **Replaces `BigButton` everywhere.**
- `IconButton`, `Card` (filled/outlined/elevated), `AppBar`, `TextField`, `Chip`, `ListItem`, `StatusPill` — composable, all consistent.

**Screen rewrites**

- **Home** — proper hierarchy. One eyebrow label ("GATE ENTRY"), one display title, one primary action ("Scan item" at `size=xl`), two tonal tiles showing live counts (deliveries / catalog) — both tappable, each tile a Card.
- **Scanner** — full-screen camera with a translucent mask that cuts out a 260×260 framed window with corner brackets and an animated green scan line. White flash + success haptic the instant a code is detected. Top bar with close + torch toggle, bottom hint. The lock is set **synchronously** before the DB lookup so subsequent frames are dropped immediately.
- **Register product** — `headlineMedium` question ("What is this?"), helper line, real `TextField` with focus state, chip rows for category/unit, sticky bottom bar with primary + text-button cancel. Keyboard-avoiding.
- **Receiving** — elevated product card with eyebrow, then a big centered display-size quantity with circular ± steppers, then quick-set pills (5/10/20/50). Mismatch shown as a tonal danger pill, not red shouting.
- **Outbox** — proper FlatList with cards, status pills, error text. Empty state with a real headline + hint.
- **Orders** — cards per order with vendor + due date + completion pill, then itemized rows with divider lines and per-item progress.

**Navigation** — theme tokens piped into `@react-navigation/native` so route transitions match. Stack uses `slide_from_right` everywhere, `fade` for the scanner.

### Scanner speed — what I tightened

The slowness on cheap Androids with `expo-camera` comes from three things; I addressed all of them:

1. **Format list reduced** — only `ean13 / ean8 / upc_a / upc_e / code128 / qr`. Every extra format adds work per frame. Memoized so the array isn't re-created on re-render.
2. **Synchronous lock + fire-and-forget DB lookup** — the moment a frame yields a barcode, we set the lock ref and fire the haptic + flash. The DB lookup runs as a Promise; navigation happens whenever it resolves. This means subsequent frames during the lookup are dropped without doing any work.
3. **Memoized barcode settings + `autofocus="on"`** — keeps the camera from rebinding the scanner on every render and forces continuous focus on Android.

**The honest ceiling**: `expo-camera`'s barcode scanner on Expo Go is fundamentally slower than ML Kit. On a worn label in dim light, even a tuned `expo-camera` will lag. To get the <200ms scans the architecture doc promised, you need to graduate from Expo Go to a custom dev build with `react-native-vision-camera` + `vision-camera-code-scanner` — that's the Phase 2 step. The current build is as fast as Expo Go gets.

### Rules of the system (so we stay consistent as it grows)

1. **Never import from `react-native` for styles directly in screens** for things tokens can express. Use `palette`, `spacing`, `radius` from `design/`.
2. **Never write inline `fontSize`**. Use `<Text variant=...>`.
3. **Buttons go through `<Button>`**. If a new shape is needed, add a variant to `Button.tsx`, don't reinvent it in the screen.
4. **One primary action per screen.** Secondary actions are `tonal` or `outlined`. Tertiary is `text`.
5. **Pressed feedback is mandatory.** All touchables either go through `Button` / `IconButton` / `Chip` (which already do it) or replicate the pattern (scale + haptic + android_ripple).

Reload Expo Go and you should see the new Home screen come up. Let me know what feels off and I'll tune the tokens.

> AGENT

SSCC is tricky because it's usually printed in one of three formats:

| Encoding | What expo-camera does | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **GS1-128** (Code 128 + FNC1) | ✅ Decodes the Code128 bars fine | But the payload comes back with the FNC1 / GS (ASCII 29) separator embedded — looks like garbage if you don't strip it |
| **GS1 DataMatrix** | ✅ Supported (`datamatrix` in our list) | Same FNC1 caveat |
| **GS1 DataBar** (Expanded / Stacked / Limited) | ❌ **Not supported by expo-camera at all** | The bundled scanner can't see these bars |

So the first thing we need to know is **which format your SSCC label is in**. The fastest way is to let the camera log whatever it actually picks up, even if we currently route it the wrong way.

Let me add diagnostic logging — every detected barcode prints its `type` and `data` to the Metro console, so you can stand in front of the label and see exactly what (if anything) is being decoded.

Strip FNC1 (ASCII 29) so the printable payload is visible.\n      const printable = data?.replace(/\\x1d/g, '|');\n      console.log('[scan]', type, JSON.stringify(printable));\n      if (lockedRef.current || !data) return;\n      lockedRef.current = true;\n      haptic.success();\n      flash();"

> AGENT

### How to diagnose

1. Reload Expo Go.
2. Open Metro in your terminal — keep it visible.
3. Open the scanner and aim at the SSCC label. Move closer / further, change angle.
4. Watch the Metro logs.

Three outcomes, three different fixes:

**A. You see `[scan] code128 "00|123456789012345678"` (or similar with `|` separators)**

The label is GS1-128. The camera is decoding it; the only "problem" is that the FNC1 separator (now shown as `|`) makes the payload look weird and the app then treats the whole string as a barcode key. Fix is a small GS1 parser that extracts the `00` SSCC application identifier. I can add that — tell me and I'll wire it in.

**B. You see `[scan] datamatrix ...`**

Same as A but DataMatrix encoded. Same parser handles both.

**C. You see nothing — no log lines at all when pointing at the label**

It's a **GS1 DataBar** (you can tell visually: GS1 DataBar Expanded looks like a stacked, segmented barcode rather than a single row of bars). expo-camera literally cannot decode this format. There are only two real fixes:

1. **Graduate to a dev build** with `react-native-vision-camera` + `vision-camera-code-scanner`. ML Kit *does* support GS1 DataBar Expanded/Stacked/Omnidirectional. This is the Phase 2 move the architecture doc already plans for.
2. If GS1 DataBar is rare in your supplier mix, accept it as a manual-entry fallback for now.

### Tell me what the logs show

Run a scan and paste the `[scan] ...` lines (or "nothing prints"). Based on that I'll either:
- Add the GS1 AI parser (5-min job), or
- Set up the dev-client + vision-camera migration (~30-min job, gets you ML Kit speed and DataBar support both).

> AGENT

If literally nothing logs even when you point straight at it, expo-camera isn't able to decode that label at all. Before we commit to a big migration, two things:

### 1. Quick diagnostic — confirm it's not a focus/framing issue

Our scan window is 260×260px. SSCC labels are often wide and short, and if the camera can't see the whole barcode (with quiet zone on either side) inside the focused region, decoding silently fails. Try this without changing code:

- Get **further back** so the whole label fits in frame, then move slowly closer.
- Make sure the lighting is bright.
- Try rotating the phone 90° — sometimes a wide 1D barcode decodes better in landscape framing even if the screen is portrait.

Also let me strip the format filter entirely so we're not accidentally excluding something:

### 2. Can you describe the barcode visually? Or send a photo?

The answer changes the migration path:

| What the label looks like | Likely format | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| One row of vertical bars, wide and short | **GS1-128** (Code 128 + FNC1) | expo-camera *should* read it; if not, vision-camera + ML Kit will |
| Square grid of dots | **GS1 DataMatrix** | expo-camera *should* read it; if not, ML Kit will |
| Stacked / segmented bars with gaps between rows, looks like 2 or 3 short barcodes stacked | **GS1 DataBar Expanded / Stacked** | **Neither expo-camera nor ML Kit support this.** Needs a commercial SDK (Dynamsoft, Scandit, Scanbot) or a ZXing-based native module |

That last row is the bad case. If your SSCC labels are GS1 DataBar, swapping to vision-camera + ML Kit won't fix it — ML Kit's barcode scanner doesn't list DataBar in its supported formats. The honest options become:

- **`react-native-vision-camera` + Dynamsoft Barcode Reader plugin** — supports DataBar, has a free tier for low volume.
- **Scanbot SDK for React Native** — supports DataBar, commercial.
- **Switch to a phone with a hardware barcode scanner** (e.g. Zebra TC21) — overkill unless this is a permanent workflow.
- **Manual entry fallback** — for the small number of DataBar items, type the 18-digit SSCC. Useful only if DataBar is rare in your supplier mix.

### What I'd like from you

1. Try the diagnostic above (full-frame, good light, rotate). Report whether **any** log lines appear.
2. If you can, send/describe what the barcode looks like — single row vs. stacked.

Once I know which of the three cases you're in, I'll set up the right migration (vision-camera + ML Kit dev build, or vision-camera + a DataBar-capable SDK). Don't want to commit you to a 30-minute native rebuild and find out it doesn't solve the problem.

> AGENT

Sorry about that detour — and that explains why nothing logged after I removed the settings (expo-camera disables scanning entirely without them). The original "no logs on SSCC" still stands though: with the full format list in place, expo-camera couldn't decode your SSCC label. Time to migrate.

### The migration plan

We move the scanner from `expo-camera` to `react-native-vision-camera` (v4), which uses **ML Kit on Android**. ML Kit is materially better at:

- Worn / damaged / off-angle 1D barcodes (Code128, EAN, UPC, ITF)
- Low-resolution captures (cheap Android sensors)
- DataMatrix at small sizes
- Multi-format frames (it tries all formats per frame, fast)

What ML Kit **also can't read** (be honest upfront):

- **GS1 DataBar Expanded / Stacked / Limited.** Not in ML Kit's supported list. If your SSCC label is in that format, even after this migration it won't decode — we'd need a commercial SDK (Dynamsoft / Scanbot / Scandit) on top of vision-camera. We'll find out after the migration whether that's required.

### What this costs you

**One-way door, mostly:** Expo Go can't load `react-native-vision-camera` (it's a native module that's not bundled into Expo Go). Once we add it, you run the app via a **custom dev build** instead. That means:

- One-time: `npx expo prebuild` generates real `ios/` and `android/` folders, then `npx expo run:android` builds and installs a dev-client APK on a connected device or emulator. Takes 5–15 minutes the first time.
- After that: same `npm start` flow, but scan the QR with the **dev-client APK** you just installed, not Expo Go.
- Requires **Android Studio installed** (for the Android SDK + build tools) or a Mac with Xcode for iOS. If you don't have either, we'd use **EAS Build** (Expo's cloud builder) — free tier covers ~30 builds/month.

**Code changes are small** — just the `ScannerScreen` file, plus app.json plugin config. Everything else stays.

### Before I touch anything — three questions

1. **Do you have Android Studio (or Xcode) installed locally?** If yes, we use `expo run:android`. If no, we use EAS Build (you'll need a free Expo account).
2. **What's the SSCC label visually?** Single row of bars (Code 128) → ML Kit will fix it. Stacked / segmented rows (DataBar) → ML Kit won't, and we should plan for a commercial SDK upfront.
3. **OK to proceed?** Once we add vision-camera, you stop using Expo Go for this project.

Once you confirm those, I'll:
- Add `react-native-vision-camera` and its Expo plugin to `package.json` and `app.json`
- Rewrite `ScannerScreen` to use `useCodeScanner` from vision-camera
- Give you the exact `prebuild` + `run:android` commands (or the EAS variant)

If after the migration ML Kit still can't read your specific SSCC label, we'll know it's GS1 DataBar and the next step is a paid SDK plugin — that I'll lay out then, not preemptively.

Good — that's **GS1-128**: standard Code 128 with an FNC1 prefix and an `(00)` Application Identifier for SSCC (18 digits). It is single-row, not DataBar.

That's the **best possible answer** because:

- It's plain Code 128 under the hood, which ML Kit handles excellently.
- The reason `expo-camera` can't decode it is density — 18 digits + FNC1 + start/stop + check makes a very dense barcode, and `expo-camera` on Android uses low-resolution preview frames for scanning.
- Vision-camera + ML Kit uses higher-resolution frames and a much better decoder. This **will** read it.

So we don't need a paid SDK — the standard migration solves it.
